1 
台積電可能與馬斯克合作興建並 
營運「Terafab」評析

報告日期： 2025/09/19 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
新 聞 評 析 
新聞重點摘要： 
台積電可能與馬斯克合作興建並營運「 Terafab 」。

一. 我們認為成案可能性極低  
我們認為台積電僅基於商業友好關係與馬斯克進行商討，但成案
的可能性極低，主因台積電表明過其晶圓廠的建置與營運都以
100% 自主擁有為主 。 雖然目前在德國的德勒斯登的ESMC 為合資
(台積電持股 70% 、博世持股 10% 、英飛凌持股 10% 、恩智浦持
股 10%) ，及在日本的熊本廠 JASM 為合資 (台積電持股 86.5% 、
Sony 持股  6.0% 、DENSO 持股 5.5% 、Toyota 持股 2.0%) ，但大
都因 2021 ～2022 年 COVID-19 期間車用晶圓持續供不應求，廠
商為了避免因半導體產能供貨不足而影響出貨的情況發生，紛紛
以能確保半導體晶圓供充足為優先，且後續接續 AI 需求蓬勃發
展，晶圓需求緊俏程度更加嚴重，各國紛紛力邀台積電到該國設
廠，日本及德國為汽車製造大國，因此，台積電於日本及德國的
設廠大都以車用的成熟製程或特殊製程為主 (僅 Sony 以 CMOS 影
像感測為主 )，在當地政府的建廠補貼下，也開放該國企業持有部
份股權。

我們認為本次傳言台積電可能與馬斯克合作興建並營運
「 Terafab 」的技術層次將會拉高到最先進製程的水準，一旦
Terafab 開始營運後，將直接與台積電的先進製程產生衝突，因
此，我們認為成案的可能性極低。馬斯克建置的晶圓廠初期的想
法也是因為晶圓代工產能持續供不應求，馬斯克將建置自有的晶
圓廠滿足旗下 Tesla 車用晶片 、Optimus 機器人 、Dojo 訓練晶片、
AI 晶片、低軌衛星晶片，尤其因目前 AI 的競爭發展白熱化，晶片
的運算能力及設計複雜度都大幅提升，使用最先進的製程節點勢
在必行，因此，馬斯克先前也曾於 2026 年 4 月宣布將與 Intel 合
作 14A 製程，導入 Terafab 進行生產製造，由於 14A 將是 Intel
下一階段最先進的製程，顯見馬斯克對最先進的製程有強烈的需
求，其 Terafab 勢必以發展最先進製程為主，因此，若台積電與
馬斯克合作，勢必也要以其最先進的製程 2nm 為主，甚至下一世
代的 16A ，才能滿足馬斯克的期待，如此，台積電先進製程機密
可能有外洩風險，且若如馬斯克宣稱的 Terafab 初期產能 10 萬片

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

2 
/月，目前將達 100 萬片 /月，屆時晶圓代工產能將有過剩問題，因
為台積電 2025 年約當 12 吋晶圓的出貨量約 1,500 萬片 ，Terafab
若達 100 萬片 /月(年產能 1,200 萬片 )，將是一個龐大的產能的䆁
出。

目前台積電 2nm+3nm+5nm 佔總營收比重約 66% ，由於 ASP 相
對較高，我們推估其出貨量僅佔總出貨量的 35% ，毛利率也高於
平均水準 (雖然 2nm 仍在進行良率調整，尚未達公司目標，但其於
4Q25 開始量產，僅僅花兩個季度就已經開始賺錢 )，在 AI 需求強
勁的帶動下，台積電也非常極積的擴充 3nm 的產能，甚至將 5nm
的設備改機成 3nm ，顯見得 5nm 以下為台積電最賺錢的製程節
點，一旦與馬斯克合作 5nm 以下的製程節點，製程機密可能外流
的風險將遠高於未來可能接單的利益。考量台積電持續為 AI 浪潮
下的主要受惠者，先進製程、先進封裝同步大幅優於競爭對手，
預估台積電 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 為 108.1/142.76 元，投資建
議為買進，目標價 3141 元(22X2027 年稅後 EPS) 。

Hon Hai (2317.TW): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 3Q 
/ 4Q26E; 3-month revenues preview; Buy (on CL)
24 September 2026 | 8:50PM HKT
We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +14% / +23% QoQ, driven by (1) AI 
data center: The next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp up, with 
higher ASP and early delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first one to deliver the 
latest model, around 2-3 months ahead of peers, driving its Sep and onward revenue 
growth. Followed by GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks 
would fuel the growth in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27, and 
(2) Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along 
with the new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlight in September 
(report link), we reduce global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m to 
1.1bn / 1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; 
however, we remain positive on global leading brands to outperform the market 
given the strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ 
stronger consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect 
premium models to continue gain market share, positive to Hon Hai. Maintain Buy 
(on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 3Q26E revenues to be +40% 
YoY/ +14% QoQ to NT$2.9 trillion. Both Jul and Aug delivered strong revenues YoY 
growth (+54%/ 52% YoY), and we expect to see both YoY and MoM positive growth 
in Sep to Dec, driven by new AI server racks, CPO switch, and latest smartphone 
models and foldable phones. We keep 2026E - 2028E earnings estimates largely 
unchanged.

### Page 3

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and we continue to 
derive it through near term PE. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 17.9x (vs. 17.8x of our 
previous TP implied 2027E P/E) is derived from peers’ average ratio of trading P/E to 
forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the sum of NI YoY and OPM). Our 
target P/E of 17.9x is in the range of the company’s historical forward P/E since 2010, 
reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from the competitive 
consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which should drive 
profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. Maintain Buy (on 
CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2)

### Page 1

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
Upgrade to Buy: Superior Margin on Tanker Fleet + High Export Ratio
CITI'S TAKE 
We upgrade FPCC from Neutral to Buy reflecting its unique exposure with 
in-house VLCC shipping fleet covering ~60-70% of oil transportation (least 
impacted by recent spot freight rate spike) and highest diesel export ratio 
( ~85% vs. 60% for S-Oil/SKI and 5-10% for TOP/SPRC). We see manageable 
impact from CPC’s pump price freeze (minimal margin squeeze at ~$80/bbl 
Dubai) while FPCC is also a beneficiary of any potential US diesel export 
restriction (Iink). We expect FPCC to offer the highest 26-27E dividend yield 
( ~ 7-8%) among 4 Formosa sisters and a premium valuation seems justified 
vs. Asian peers. We think oil product tightness might take longer to resolve 
given extensive Russia refinery outages. New TP at NT$108 (from NT$40). 
Quantifying impact of domestic price cap — Taiwan’s CPC indicates its retail price 
absorption reached NT$22bn cumulative YTD (Fig.1) vs. NT$17bn in end-Jun and we 
estimate ~NT$7bn in Sep alone (similar to Mar peak). FPCC has 22% Taiwan market 
share and 2025 domestic gasoline & diesel volume was 35k/17k b/d or 12% of CDU 
runs subject to price control (vs. ~18% for S-Oil/SKI, 35% for Thai peers). Assuming 
oil price stays at $100 until end-4Q, we estimate 2H26 domestic forgone EBIT could 
be similar to 1H ( ~NT$5bn) or ~US$2/bbl overall GRM drag. But this was more than 
compensated by SG product crack strength with 2025 gasoline/diesel/jet export 
ratios of 59%/86%/99%, respectively.   
Buffered from current $20+ spot VLCC rate — FPCC is one of the very few refiners 
that owns vessels (through 20% stake in Formosa Plastics Marine that has 6 VLCCs). 
We expect it could enjoy stable freight cost under LT time chartering. We also see 
lower concern on crude sourcing from SoH disruption in 3Q noting Jul/Aug refinery 
runs were 88%/92%. But we see pullback into 4Q (mid-70%) on maintenance. 
Improved olefin competitiveness from 2027 — We expect FPCC could raise cracker 
utilisation next year (33% average since 2Q) after partial conversion to ethane feed 
(up to ~400kt pa usage or ~20% of output) with minimal capex. 
Implications — Our revised TP of NT$108 is based on historical peak 2.3x P/BV (core 
book) at ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil given limited free float and MTM for 
affiliates (its 7.9% stake in Nanya Tech is worth NT$14/share based on market price). 
Our sensitivity analysis suggests a $5 diesel crack rise vs. our mid-$30/bbl base 
case will raise FY27E EPS by ~10%.

Oscar Yee AC

In Taiwan, state-owned Chinese Petroleum Corp (CPC) announces retail price
adjustment on a weekly basis based on government’s fuel pricing formula (linked to
70% Dubai / 30% Brent crude basket adjusted for exchange rate) but with higher
absorption amount as oil goes higher. FPCC tends to follow suit. CPC’s domestic
price absorption was minimal in Jun-Jul’26 when Dubai price falls to ~$80/bbl or

below. However, there was a price freeze last week despite crude basket surged
another $10/bbl WoW to $126/bbl.

### Page 7

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
25 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Formosa Petrochemical
Company description
Formosa Petrochemical (FPCC) is part of the Formosa Group in Taiwan. It
owns a 540,000 b/d refinery, 2.9m tonnes of ethylene capacity from three
naphtha crackers, and a 2,800MW cogeneration power plant. It was set up
when the Taiwan government approved expansion of the private sector into
the oil refining and upstream chemical business.
Investment strategy

We rate Formosa Petrochemical stock a Buy. Despite a likely gradual GRM
normalisation ahead (dependent on Middle East / Russia supply disruptions),
we expect oil product tightness might take longer to resolve vs. crude oil
market. Its gasoline/diesel export ratio was the highest among Asian peers,
which should more than buffer margin squeeze from domestic pump control.
FPCC was the least impacted by recent spot freight rate spike given in-house
VLCC shipping fleet. While we remain concerned about an L-shaped ethylene

cycle in the next 2-3 years, FPCC could enjoy better cost competitiveness as
~20% of olefin cracker feedstock will shift to ethane from early-2027 with
minimal capex. The LT refining outlook could remain solid due to permanent
closures in the US/Europe. Healthy dividend yield (likely to be the highest
among 4 Formosa sisters in FY26E), stable utility earnings and the stock's
relatively low free float (15%) vs. its peers could provide further support.

Valuation
Our NT$108 target price for Formosa Petrochemical is based on historical
peak-cycle 2.3x FY27E P/BV with adjusted book value excluding valuation
gain of affiliates, ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil implied from our
TP given limited free float and MTM for affiliates. We use P/BV as our
valuation methodology due to the asset-heavy nature of the business, and
factor in its scarcity premium vs, regional peers as the only energy play in
Taiwan.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected oil and/or chemical demand; and 2)
Taiwan government intervention on CPC's oil products pricing mechanism.
Key upside risks are: 1) extended refinery outages in the Middle East / Russia,
2) stronger global oil demand growth; 3) robust chemical re-stocking in
China; and 4) a short-term spike in refining margins on any unscheduled
shutdowns or for weather-related reasons (e.g., cold winter).

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Compal (2324.TW): AI servers to fuel 4Q26E/1Q27E growth; Aug miss; 
Neutral
27 September 2026 | 6:06PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
considering the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, 
and PC product mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect 
Compal’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 1% / 16% QoQ, riding on AI server racks 
ramp up and the company’s expansion from motherboard (L6) to system (L10 / L11). 
We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, supported by global 
capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, Vietnam and China, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, for which 
demand could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Compal’s Aug revenue was up 15% YoY / down 17% MoM to NT$67bn, or 20% lower 
than our estimate, reflecting PC shipments decline. Aug PC shipments was down 22% 
MoM to 1.4m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising 
memory cost and weak consumer electronics demand.

Earnings revision: Factoring in Compal’s Aug revenue, we cut our 2026-28E net

income by 5% / 1% / 1% to reflect the miss, mainly on lower revenues and higher opex

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12-month TP based on near-term P/E. Our 2027E 
target P/E is updated to 14.7x (vs. 13.1x previously), which is derived from the refreshed 
peers’ correlation of forward trading P/E and EPS growth. With our updated earnings 
estimates and target multiple, our 12-month TP is raised to NT$46.1 (vs. NT$41.3 
previously). The target P/E of 14.7x is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.+1std 
forward trading P/E. Maintain Neutral.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$46.1. Our target 
price is based on 14.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Aspeed

Growth profile remains robust into 2027; 
reiterate Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 26,000 TWD | Price: 19,835 TWD

Looming price hike and smoother supply 
Aspeed’s (see report) 4Q26 revenue guidance in our view should incorporate 20% price 
hike across the board including AST2700 BMC (priced at ~US$30 after the hike), mainly 
reflecting the cost inflation from OSAT (outsourced semiconductor assembly/testing) 
vendor. Yet with incremental supply secured across foundry, OSAT, substrate and glass 
cloth, the firm expects a normalized DoI (days of inventory) level of ~12 weeks from 
2Q27.   
Sustained leadership with value-add in security 
Management continues to underscore around 2-year technology lead vs its main 
competitor in the merchant market, while the adoption of Caliptra 2.2 standard in 
AST2750 BMC further enhanced its value-add in network security. We deem this a 
positive to (see report) eMemory given the share gain in PUF business against its 
counterpart in US, from which Aspeed adopts Caliptra 1.1 in AST2700 BMC.   
Rosy demand growth outlook for 2027 
Looking into 2027, Aspeed cites 50-60% growth for BMC applied to GPU and ASIC 
markets, while there could be 1) an even stronger growth in Agentic AI (artificial 
intelligence); and 2) 6-10% YoY in general server (w.o. Agentic AI in our view). Given such 
demand strength, the company may provide an updated version of the BMC TAM (total 
addressable market) by end-2026.   
Flag recent positives from the industry; re-it. Buy 
Recently we see positives to server CPU (central processing unit) supply chain from (see 
reports) Meta’s Muse and expert call takeaways which cite continuous share gain by 
ARM-based solutions. Reiterate Buy on Aspeed given its unrivalled position in a 
structurally growing market.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Aspeed was founded in 2004 as a fabless company, 
focusing on the design of baseboard management 
controller (BMC), which could be used in remote servers. 
Aspeed has 70+% market share in BMC, while it also offers 
other products including PC/AV extension IC and digital 
image processors.

Investment Rationale 
We rate Aspeed as Buy, as we anticipate stable growth in 
the regular server market into 2026-27, along with its 
growing exposure to AI server market. Outside of server 
BMC, we also like its increasing traction in new products 
(mini-BMC, root of trust, etc.) in the server area. Further, we 
see stronger growth in non-server business led mainly by 
Cupola 360.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 60.5x

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Aspeed (XLKMF) 
Our DCF-based PO is NT$26,000, and we believe the valuation is justified by (1) the 
firm's high market share in the server BMC market, (2) secular growth in overall server 
market, despite near-term ebbs and flows of orders and shipments, (3) nearly 60% 
earnings CAGR in 2026-28E, and (4) about NT$215 net cash/share on hand, we assume 
WACC of 7% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Upside risk to our PO are 
(1) stronger-than-expected operating leverage on stronger revenue growth, and (2) 
faster progress in ramping up non-BMC product lines including Cupola 360.

Downside risks to our PO are 
(1) softer server (thus BMC) demand due to macro uncertainties, or pressured by trade 
conflict 
(2) market-share loss in BMC to current/potential competitors such as Nuvoton 
(3) customers' market-share loss in ones (such as HP and Dell) to which Aspeed has less 
exposure.

## Document 070: 260928_ubs_n2-TAM

Source PDF: all report pdf/260928_ubs_n2-TAM.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
UBS Global I/O Semiconductors
Foundry: Much larger N2 TAM and faster A14
expansion; mature foundry upcycle underway
N2 demand inflection larger than expected
Our proprietary bottoms-up N2 foundry TAM analysis points to substantially larger
demand of 320kwpm by 2028E, versus our prior estimate of approximately 230kwpm a
year ago. The increase is driven primarily by stronger server CPU demand and
accelerating AI compute deployments, particularly as a growing number of ASIC

programs ramp into volume production alongside GPUs over the next several years. At
the same time, we  expect Intel to migrate a larger portion of its PC CPU production to
18A as execution improves. As a result, we estimate server CPUs and accelerators will
account for 54% of N2 foundry demand by 2028, up from our previous estimate of
44%, while PCs should contribute 19% and smartphones/tablets 26%. (See Figure 6Q u a n t if y in g  N 2  d e m a n d  o p p o r t u n it ie s  f o r  f o u n d r y  -

Figure 7N 2  f o u n d r y  d e m a n d  b y  a p p lic a t io n  (2 0 2 8 E )). The larger N2 opportunity supports robust revenue growth for TSMC through
2028-29E as the industry's major foundry supplier, even as competition from Samsung
Foundry, Intel, and Terafab increases modestly.
Further acceleration of N3/2 expansion; quicker A14 capacity build-up
To meet the larger N2 demand outlook, we raise our estimate for industry N2 capacity at
end-2028 to 312kwpm, up from 230kwpm previously. We forecast TSMC's N2 capacity

reaching 210kwpm in 2028, versus our prior estimate of 160kwpm. Intel should also
scale capacity more aggressively, exceeding 60kwpm in 2028E for internal CPU
production, compared with our previous forecast of 40kwpm. Meanwhile,
strengthening cloud AI demand in 2027-28E is pushing our industry N3 capacity
forecast to 290-300kwpm by 2028, up from 270kwpm previously, driven entirely by
faster expansion at TSMC. TSMC's A14 node remains on track for mass production in

2028, supported by steady R&D progress and growing customer engagement. Industry
feedback suggests TSMC is pulling forward its expansion schedule, targeting
approximately 60kwpm of A14 capacity in 2028 when the node enters mass
production, based on  UBSe. Reflecting the more aggressive leading-edge expansion, we
raise our TSMC capex forecast to US$90bn in 2027 and US$105bn in 2028, up from US
$63bn this year. See our Q326 earnings preview.
Mature foundry: favorable supply/demand and pricing outlook in 2027-28

We were early in advocating the anti-consensus view of a mature foundry upcycle,
driven by an industry-wide supply and resource reallocation. Utilization and pricing
trends have continued to improve throughout 2026, and we expect more meaningful
earnings upside in 2027-28. Our updated analysis suggests 8-inch foundry utilization
could rise to 95% in 2027, up from 85% in 2026, supported by growing server PMIC
demand. While 12-inch 28/40nm demand may face near-term headwinds from weaker

smartphone and consumer demand, we still expect utilization to improve to 88% in
2027 from 84% in 2026, supported by modest capacity growth. We also believe
competitive behaviour from Chinese foundries should remain rational into 2027, as
major players increasingly prioritize capital allocation toward leading-edge technologies.
Stock recommendations
We reiterate Buy on TSMC and expect further upward revisions to Street revenue, EPS
and capex estimates for 2027-28. Within mature foundry, UMC remains our top pick, as

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
The semiconductor industry is cyclically sensitive to the economy and so upside and downside
risks relate to macro drivers. Other, semiconductor capital equipment specific downside risks
include the rapid loss of market share due to product timing/performance , design or
distribution. The supply chain companies into the handset industry also operate in a highly
cyclical market both from macro concerns and inventory cycles within the supply chain. These

companies also operate in a highly competitive environment with product innovation and
R&D investments critical to success.

Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 076: 260929_ms_TDK-Taiyo-Yuden

### Page 1

M
Update
Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
TDK-Taiyo Yuden MOU: high-
end wall rises; cycle thesis
intact
The MOU validates AI-driven high-cap MLCC tightness, which
supports pricing and our MLCC up-cycle view.
TDK and Taiyo Yuden (both covered by Shoji Sato) signed an MOU to explore an
alliance in advanced MLCCs/inductors –  including manufacturing/procurement
cooperation, potential METI subsidies under Japan's Economic Security Act, and a
possible future capital tie-up.

Our take: We estimate MLCC demand next year at more than 40bn units of 47uF+,
which crowds out  midrange/low-end MLCC capacity.
1. We believe  de-specing to ≤22uF MLCCs is not a viable workaround.
Substituting high-cap MLCCs with larger counts of lower-capacitance parts
requires broad real estate that simply does not exist on today's densely
packed GPU/accelerator boards.
2. Switching to tantalum-polymer capacitors merely trades one shortage for
another, given equally tight tantalum supply.

3. So the only realistic industry response, in our view, is reallocating midrange/
low-end MLCC capacity toward high-cap production. That would structurally
tightens midrange/low-end supply, underpinning broad-based pricing power.
Things to watch:
• Whether a capital alliance materializes,
• METI subsidy size (this determines how distorted the high-end cost curve
gets),
• Counter-move by Murata (also covered by Shoji Sato) counter-move, and
• Any sign the alliance extends into mid-cap products.

See our latest report on MLCC here: Global Technology: MLCC: Continued Demand
Growth for High-Value-added Products for AI & Data Centers (15 Sep 2026)

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 081: 260930_ml_ASE

### Page 1

ASE Technology Holding

Continued Capex spree underpins strong 
business outlook

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 800.00 TWD | Price: 703.00 TWD

YTD equipment buys tracks 82% of its '26 capex guidance 
ASE has filed 152 equipment buys and 101 facility construction announcements through 
late September. The spending on equipment YTD has reached NT$273bn, tracking 82% 
of its '26 capex guidance of US$10.5bn (has revised up from US$8.5bn) not yet 
considering their land and facility purchase. The equipment buy should mainly be for 
2.5D packaging and chip probing, reflecting accelerated capacity expansion amid strong 
demand for its LEAP business from its own project wins and its foundry partner’s 
outsourcing. To secure sufficient facility infrastructure to house additional capacity, ASE 
has announced NT$112bn of spending YTD on facility and clean room construction 
contracts, with these projects expected to progressively complete through 2027-2028.  
Solid sales/margins across the board sponsor heavy capex 
Following ASE’s July announcements to acquire new lands and facilities in Kaohsiung and 
Taoyuan, the company announced another NT$2.15bn facility acquisition in Singapore to 
support the future testing expansion needs in its Singapore subsidiary as its major 
customers request supply base diversification. Despite rising depreciation from heavy 
capex investments and additional funding needs, we forecast ASE’s IC ATM sales to 
+37% CAGR from ‘26-28 while GMs should improve to 33.8%/37% in ‘27/28 (from 
28.3% in 2026) on solid packaging and testing demand for AI chipsets (BofAe LEAP 
sales US$4/10/13bn in 2026/27/28) and fast improving mainstream business outlook.  
Decent earnings support re-rating despite funding needs 
We keep Buy on ASE with NT$800 PO on 20x 2H27-1H28 P/E and maintain EPS for 
'26/27/28E at NT$18/31/45. While additional funding may be required to finance its 
aggressive spending on expansion and R&D projects, we believe the decent customer 
commitment and ROE of 25-30%+ from LEAP business should justify the funding costs.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Established in 1984 and headquartered in Taiwan, ASE 
Technology Holding is the no.1 outsourced assembly and 
testing (OSAT) firm globally, in terms of market share by 
revenue. ASE acquired Siliconware Precision (SPIL) in 2018, 
and had acquired USI in 2010, which became its EMS 
business segment providing synergies in system-level 
packaging. 2 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
We rate ASE Buy, factoring in the company's opportunity in 
advanced packaging and testing evidenced in TSMC's 
outsourcing and its own heavy capex investment. The 
strategy shift should allow the company to capture fast 
growing addressable market in high performance 
computing applications and improves its margins. ASE 
could maintain its leading position in the Outsourced 
Semiconductor Assembly and Test (OSAT) universe, owing 
to its well-established footprints and widespread service 
offerings.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
ASE Technology Holding (XSRIF / ASX) 
We value ASE Technology Holding at NT$800 per share (US$51 for ADR), based on 20x 
2H27-1H28E P/E, above its historical range of 5-26x, in view of its solidified industry 
position in AI era. We use P/E ratio to value ASE as the company has been able to 
maintain healthy profitability through the cycles in the past 20 years due to 
consolidating industry landscape supporting a more benign supply/demand and keeping 
pricing and margins less cyclical.

Downside risks to our PO are 1) share loss and/or ASP erosion owing to Chinese players' 
cannibalization and/or ASE's worsening execution, 2) weakening end demand due to 
uncontrollable matters, such as macro halt and/or geopolitical tensions, 3) regulatory 
issues that could hinder ASE from generating synergies and benefit its competitors.

Alliance(2025/2-2030/1)。安裝脫硫塔比重超過 6 成，自有櫃佔 54%。1H26 年
美洲線佔營收比重 36%，歐洲線佔 39%，近洋佔 13%，其他航線(中東、南美等)
佔 12%，運送量 223.6 萬 TEU(+4%YoY)，平均運價 1012 美元/TEU(-5%YoY)。
股權方面，官股持有 31%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元：受惠美洲線與亞洲線運價上漲，加上 LNG 船投
入地中海航線 ， 預估營收656 億元(+56%YoY)， 稅後獲利187.5 億元(+210%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元。4Q 歐洲線進入淡季、美洲線受巴拿馬運河水位降低相對有
撐、亞洲進入旺季加上塞港相對強勢，公司認為4Q 呈現緩步修正，研究部預估營
收 573 億元(+53%YoY)，稅後獲利 136.4 億元(+496%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 3.91
元。

整體燃油成本較去年同期+1%。2Q 高硫油平均 617 美元/噸(+23.5%QoQ)，低硫
油平均 775 美元/噸(+24.5%QoQ)，因地緣政治影響，3Q 高硫油平均 562 美元/
噸-9%QoQ)，低硫油平均 792 美元/噸(+2.3%QoQ)，研究部假設陽明年耗油量

2026 年供給成長是近幾年最低一年：巴拿馬運河當局 9/4 將每天通行艘樹從 36
艘降至 34 艘，9/15 再降到 32 艘，目前運河水位較去年低 2.5%，預期 10 月水位
將低於去年同期 3.8%，艙位供給將再度限縮。無論是 Drewry，Clarkson 或是
Alphaliner 預估 2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的 ， 加上紅海改道的船舶回
歸延遲，有效運力釋放再度放緩，2026 年是供給相對較小的一年。 
3Q26 第 2 艘雙燃料船交船：新船在手訂單 23 艘約 30 萬 TEU，1Q26-4Q26 年

## Document 090: 261005_daiwa_TW-CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/261005_daiwa_TW-CCL.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: After market hours on 5 October 2026, EMC, TUC, and ITEQ released their 
monthly sales results. Our read-through is positive as the three key CCL vendors beat 
sales expectations by 5-15% on better pricing and on-going spec upgrades. Our thesis is 
that the strong AI demand is causing a spill-over impact on the whole CCL industry with 
low-end CCL having c.100% price hike in 2026E and high-end CCL seeing a 40-50% price 
hike due to cost inflation in 2026E. Recent data points showed this thesis remains valid. 
Looking ahead, we expect high-end/low-end CCL to see additional c.10%/20% ASP 
increases in 4Q26E, which could be the key share price catalyst. We believe that recent 
negatives news (including the impact of PTFE and CPO/NPO on the industry) are 
overdone. The impact on high-end CCL from these new technologies is likely to be limited 
from the point of view of Taiwan CCL companies. As such, we remain positive that the 
three names in our CCL coverage will ride on the decent price hike trend given favourable 
supply-demand dynamics.

Copper clad laminate (CCL) sector: Sep-26 monthly sales (TWD bn) 
PER Company Ticker Price Rating Sep 
Sales MoM YoY 3Q26

TUC 6274 TT 1,780.0 Buy 6.1 4% 115% 17.9 26% 123% 103% 49.0 22.2 7-17x 5-Oct 17.1 105%

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts. Note: Closing prices as of 5 October 2026.

• EMC (2383 TT, TWD5,700, Buy [1]) 
EMC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD21.5bn (+7% MoM, +169% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD60.9bn; +29% QoQ, +142% YoY) reaching 112% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD54.4bn) and 110% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD55.5bn). We attribute 
the revenue strength to the higher-than-expected price hike and on-going spec upgrades 
(leading to higher ASPs). Management attributes c.50% of the MoM increase in revenue in 
September to the price hike and the other 50% came to product mix optimisation. Looking 
ahead, the company doesn’t rule out the potential to increase its ASP again in 4Q26 (after 
the first time in 2Q26) in order to reflect rising raw material prices since 2Q26. As such, we 
expect revenue to grow at least high teens again on the potential price head as well as 
ongoing spec upgrades.

EMC sees limited impact from PTFE and HC glass-fibre-free CCL. Based on our 
understanding, PTFE is still under certification accounting for only limited layers inside 
specified high-end PCB as of now. As such, its impact on the high-end CCL TAM should be 
limited. As for HC glass-fibre-free CCL, the rigidity of PCB using this type of CCL could be 
a key concern to us.

Lastly, regarding the recent Co-Packaged Optics/Near-Packaged Optics (CPO/NPO) 
news’s negative impact on the CCL TAM, we also believe it to be limited given the likely 
adoption date for CPO of 2030, based on EMC’s forecast. Besides, we believe the PCB 
board inside CPO scale-up switches at that time would still need to support an 800G/1.6T 
transmission speed (or even lower) which would still require high-end PCB transmission 
although the highest transmission bandwidth can increase to 3.2T via optics. Lastly, EMC 
believes the spec of CCL won’t downgrade to M4 in the worst-case scenario but would be 
at least M6. Last but not least, we believe this kind of CPO-connected AI server might only

exist in the most of high-end scenario for training purposes only. Thus, the total volume 
shouldn’t be large.

Given its leading position in the CCL industry and the likely imbalance between supply and 
demand in 2027, we remain positive view on EMC. We have a Buy (1) rating on EMC, with 
a 12-month TP of TWD7,200, based on a target PER of 38x applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected AI server demand.

• TUC (6274 TT, TWD1,780, Buy [1]) 
TUC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD6.1bn (+4% MoM, +115% YoY), which led to its 
3Q26 sales (TWD17.9bn; +26% QoQ, +123% YoY) reaching 103% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD17.4bn) and 105% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD17.1bn). Looking 
ahead, we expect TUC to deliver at least QoQ growth in 4Q26 given 1) a price hike along 
with its peers for high-end CCL (eg. M8) and 2) some shipment delay from 3Q26 given 
flooding in Southeast Asia. For the longer term, the company is promising a continual spec 
upgrade trend in 2027 with new projects using M9-grade CCL (including low dk1, low dk2, 
and Q-glass version) in 2027 for high-end switches, AI servers, and optical transceivers. 
Considering the insufficient capacity in the CCL industry, we expect TUC to further optimise 
its product mix after acquiring more high-end CCL orders in future quarters (vs. a single 
major AI server client in 2026). We have a Buy (1) rating on TUC, with a 12-month TP of 
TWD2,320, based on a target PER of 35x applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Key 
downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected demand for switches/AI servers, and 2) market 
share loss to CCL peers.

• ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD751, Buy [1]) 
ITEQ’s September 2026 revenue was TWD5.8bn (+15% MoM, +111% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD15.6bn; +35% QoQ, +102% YoY) reaching 114% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD13.7bn) and 108% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD14.5bn). The number 
for 3Q26 was higher than our estimate due likely to a stronger-than-expected price hike 
(c.30% in 3Q26 vs. expectation of 20%). Looking ahead to 4Q26, we expect the company 
to further raise its pricing by 20-30% to factor in cost inflation in raw materials (E-glass) 
and the supply-demand gap. The company also expects the rising supply of e-glass (which 
could lift its utilisation rate from the current level of 75%) to lift its gross margin more 
significantly. For 2027, the company is excited about the new CPU adoption driving rising 
M7 CCL usage and is working on M9 certification with new end customers, which could 
become a key growth driver in 2028. We have a Buy (1) rating on ITEQ. Key downside 
risk: weaker-than-expected regular server demand.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

UMC (2303.TW) From T o

TSMC (2330.TW) From T o
Price Target NT$2,988.00 NT$3,088.00
We think the capex budget might gradually shift to AI compute
and AI networking in 2028, as memory usage needs to be
optimized. We see positives for TSMC and UMC.
TSMC 3Q26 earnings preview: We expect the company to raise full-year revenue
guidance to the low-to-mid-40% range (vs.  above 40%) Y/Y, implying 4Q26 revenue

will rise 10%-15% Q/Q amid very strong 3nm demand. 3Q26 gross margin should
reach the high end of guidance of 67.5% However, should Intel focus on internal
capacity expansion, we think TSMC would maintain 2nm capacity expansion at the
same pace and its capex at US$54bn for 2026. For 2027, we see TSMC’s capex
reaching US$75bn to US$80bn.
In terms of 2027 wafer price hikes, we see leading edge (7nm and below) up 5%-10%
and mature nodes up 0%-3%; hence, gross margin could approach 70% in 2027. As a

result, we raise  our TSMC price target  by 3% to NT$3,088.
Key topics for upcoming TSMC 3Q26 earnings call on October 15:
1. Would TSMC consider working with TereFab  (Taiwan News, Oct 3 2026) and
building new fab sites in Texas (Bloomberg, Oct 1 2026)? Our view is that expansion
in Texas could be necessary, considering customer demand and limited space at the
Arizona fab in five years.
2. If Intel were to increase capex, would that reduce outsourcing at TSMC? Our

recent industry checks suggest some trimming of Intel’s 3nm and 2nm; how might
TSMC reallocate its resources?
3. How would TSMC address large reticle size chip demand? Our checks suggest
MediaTek handled TPU v10 IceFish reticle size initially at 14x; could TSMC’s CoWoS
or CoPoS be able to support such packaging?
We raise  our UMC price target by 36% to NT$188 on AI networking (SiPh)
business upside: Some investors remain on the sidelines amid ongoing smartphone

and PC demand weakness. We have seen some order cuts in PC semis recently, as
some consumer analog IC customers (e.g., Silergy) have not been able to pass
through UMC’s wafer price hike. Therefore, some investors do not expect any UMC’s
price hike in 2027. However, two growth drivers could help UMC raise wafer price
for 8-inch in 2027: 1) Samsung DDI outsourcing, as we noted in our earlier  Foundry
Floorplan, and 2) growing SiPh PIC demand, in particular after UMC introduced its

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
October 5, 2026 08:00 PM GMT

TSMC 2nm and 3nm capacity growth remains robust; 5nm
set to decline
Consistent with our demand forecast, TSMC indicated very strong capacity requirements
for both 2nm and 3nm starting in 2026, implying a 70% capacity CAGR for N2 from 2026

to 2028. TSMC also suggested that 5nm capacity will begin to decline from 2027, in line
with our forecast that 5nm demand will drop as Blackwell transitions to Rubin on 3nm.
Separately, advanced packaging capacity expansion should remain in focus for TSMC in
2027. This includes, but is not limited to, CoWoS and SoIC, for which we now expect
capacity to reach 200kwpm and 40kwpm, respectively, by 2027.

170kwpm in 2027, and exceed 200kwpm in 2028. We expect 1.4nm to begin pulling in

tools in 2027, with the bulk of capacity ramping in 2028. Looking further ahead, we expect
TSMC to introduce initial 1.0nm capacity in Tainan in 2029. We have not baked in potential

How might TSMC address Intel's competition in both wafer
foundry and advanced packaging?
Intel EMIB-T remains competitive in 2029; how TSMC will
address?
With demand in AI continuing to boom, we expect TSMC and its OSAT partners to
expedite capacity expansion plans for CoWoS. We see TSMC's CoWoS capacity building
further to 260kwpm in 2028, from 130/200kwpm in 2026/2027, with 2028's expansion
mainly focused in Arizona's AP9/10 and potentially in AP7. TSMC is now conducting trial

production for CoPoS in Taoyuan. If the progress goes well, we would expect TSMC to
start building its CoPoS capacity, with location also likely to be in AP9/10 or AP7P3.
We expect the non-TSMC camp to expand CoWoS capacity to 110kwpm by 2028 year-end,
from 80kwpm by end-2027. For ASE/SPIL, we expect FoCoS+CoWoS capacity to rise from
30kwpm at 2027-end to 50-60kwpm at 2028-end, with expansion focused on CoWoS-L
and CoWoS-R. For Amkor, we expect CoWoS capacity to increase from 30kwpm at 2027-

end to 55-60kwpm at 2028-end, also focused on CoWoS-L and CoWoS-R.

What about  Intel EMIB capacity build?
For Intel, we see current average EMIB-M capacity of 110kwpm falling slightly to 95kwpm
in 2027 as it converts some toward EMIB-T. We see the major customers of EMIB-M being
Trainium3, upcoming Trainium 4 and its in-house server CPU. Average EMIB-T capacity is

around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale to 15-20kwpm in 2027 and 40-
45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is converted for  12-inch wafer
equivalent, which we believe would be much more easier to communicate with compared
to TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacity during 2027-28. We maintain our view that Intel EMIB-T

Indeed, we have seen some TSMC customers surveying (testing vehicles) Intel EMIB-T due
to the design for larger chip size, lower cost, and intention to seek a second source
because TSMC's CoWoS capacity is still relatively tight. However, we believe TSMC could
still try to expand its packaging size by leveraging different technologies to meet
customers' needs. We think it remains concerned about whether it is going to open its
front-end computing die production/know-how to its competitor, Intel, for back-end

packaging.
Our prediction of the trend after 2028 –  TSMC might:
1. Pull in CoPoS (panel-level process) with both 300mm and 500mm square panel
size
2. Introduce 3.5D packaging with SoIC for vertical chip stacking (Meta MTIA is
surveying)
3. Adopt glass core substrate to solve warpage for >9.7x reticle-size chips

Pros and cons of TSMC's CoWoS, Intel's EMIB, TSMC's
CoPoS, and SoIC
TSMC’s CoWoS, Intel’s EMIB, TSMC’s CoPoS, and TSMC’s SoIC represent different points
along the advanced packaging spectrum, each optimizing for a different balance of
bandwidth, cost, scalability and integration complexity. We think future design of the
chips is likely to combine these approaches—for example, using CoWoS for lateral

integration and SoIC for vertical stacking—to achieve optimal system-level performance.
Exhibit 12: Comparison between TSMC's CoWoS, CoPoS, SoIC, and Intel's EMIB-T

In summary,

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
• CoWoS excels in high-bandwidth 2.5D integration but is costly and capacity-limited
• EMIB offers a more cost-effective and modular alternative with some performance
trade-offs
• CoPoS targets large-scale, cost-efficient integration but is still maturing
• SoIC delivers the highest performance through 3D stacking at the expense of
complexity and thermal constraints.

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
UMC is well positioned for Silicon Photonic IC (PIC) foundry
In our previous Foundry Floorplan, we presented a constructive view on UMC given its AI-
related revenue. Management indicated AI revenue of US$300mn this year (3-5% of
revenue), potentially more than tripling to US$1bn in three years (2029).
UMC has a  capacity expansion plan to capture AI demand, including a clean room for
advanced packaging for AI chips, such as wafer-on-wafer and memory-on-memory stacking.

Some investors remain on the sidelines amid ongoing smartphone and PC demand
weakness, and we have seen some order cuts of PC semis recently. Some think a UMC
price hike in 2027 wouldn’t go through, but we see two growth drivers helping it
successfully  hike wafer price for 8-inch in 2027: 1) Samsung DDI outsourcing, and 2)
growing SiPh PIC demand, in particular after UMC introduced its 400Gb per lane solution
(PIC + TFLN) solution.
How UMC is differentiated in PIC foundry

According to UMC, InP and TFLN are the two most promising solutions, as they are much
more mature than the other contenders. InP is facing severe shortage, adding to TFLN’s
momentum.
UMC Group’s TFLN program started in 2021 with 6” in mass production and 8” in
sampling. TFLN's   strengths include low loss, high bandwidth (> 110 GHz), good thermal
stability, and strong linearity.
Integrating TFLN with PIC is a challenge as well as an opportunity. Advanced packaging

schemes and micro transfer printing are both seen as promising candidates. UMC is
developing the integration technology, leveraging its expertise in PIC, TFLN, and advanced
packaging.
Adoption of higher-performance modulators (TFLN/InP) and improvement in photo diodes
are key factors affecting deployment of plain-vanilla (non-WDM, IM/DD) DCIs with 400
Gbps/channel performance and beyond, which is very close to TSMC's mainstream PIC
technology Micro Ring.

Exhibit 15: UMC differentiates in TFLN process offering for PIC
Source: UMC

About Foundry Floorplan
In 2011,  we first published this product (Asia/Pacific Foundry: The Foundry Floorplan) to
provide our analysis of foundry industry dynamics. We have resumed this practice as
global foundry competition remains active. Foundry supply is also a critical resource for
future AI chips, whether in the global market or China's local market.
Below are prior reports from this series:
• The Foundry Floorplan: All boats riding on the same tide – mature node up-cycle

begins (19 May 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB competition (14
Apr 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: Raise TSMC's 2027e and 2028e Capex (9 Mar 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: Update on TSMC's 2027 capex; interposer outsourcing to
Vanguard? (13 Jan 2026)
• Asia/Pacific Foundry: The Foundry Floorplan, April 2011

TSMC: Estimate and price target revisions
Our earnings forecasts increase 4% for 2026,  5% for  2027 and 3% for 2028: We raise
our 2026 revenue forecast and modestly increase our margin assumptions to reflect
stronger-than-expected 3Q26 performance, driven by robust AI demand, including
sustained Blackwell shipments. We raise our 2027–28 revenue assumptions by 3%–5%,
reflecting the increase in strong leading-edge demand as well as the wafer price hike.

Our PT increases to NT$3,088 from   NT$2,988, driven primarily by our estimate

revisions. Our PT implies a 20x P/E multiple on our new 2027 EPS forecast. TSMC
currently trades at 17x that estimate, close to its average NTM P/E of 16.5x since 2018. We
find the stock attractive at current levels.
We continue to derive our 12-month price target (base-case scenario) using a residual
income valuation framework. Our key assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of
equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of 2.0%), an

intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 4%.
Our bull and bear case scenario values increase to NT$3,720 (from NT$3,600) and NT
$1,700 (from NT$1,645), respectively.

UMC: Estimate and price target revisions
We raise our 2026, 2027, and 2028 earnings estimates by 56%, 25%, and 31%,
respectively: Our 2026e change is due mostly to strong 2Q26 non-op gains. We increase
2027/28e revenue and EPS due to new CPO related business contribution from 12-inch
SiPh PIC  as well as 8-inch TFLN. Our 2028e increase also includes DDI outsource from
Samsung foundry.

target (base case scenario value) from a residual income model, as we believe it best
represents the value of the company. Besides our estimate increases, we raise our
intermediate growth rate from 12.5% to 13.5% due to structural changes in the industry's
supply/demand dynamics. Other key assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of

rises from NT$70 to  NT$95, implying 7.5x and 2.6x 2027e BVPS, respectively.

MS Rating
▪ We are OW as we see structural changes
in the mature node industry's
supply/demand dynamics.
▪ Its foundry quality is comparable to
TSMC's in mature nodes. Along with

specialty technologies, this could help offset
part of the margin downside.
▪ Non-AI market recovery remains uncertain
in 2027, yet UMC should still beneﬁt from
strong AI-related demand spillover from its
foundry peers.
▪ 12nm FinFET development is strategic. It
should be one of UMC's long-term
strengths, notably when Chinese foundries
cannot effectively expand FinFET capacity.
▪ The stock now trades at 4.1x 2027e BVPS.
That is well above its historical average

since 2009, but our price target implies 5.1x.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部維持奇鋐買進投資建議，並上修目標價，主係：(1) 4Q26 隨著
VR200 小量出貨，AWS T3 水冷及Google TPU 同步放量，營收與獲利
將逐季走高；(2)預期 AWS 及 AMD 將採用雙面水冷版，單價大幅提升；
(3)ASIC 產品毛利率高於GPU， 且預估27 年水冷營收比重將超過 5 成；
(4) NVIDIA CDU 認證名單及 Switch tray 規格的市場雜音，對基本
面影響有限。綜上所述，研究部上修 27 年營收至 3,409 億元(原估
3,144 億元)，EPS 上修至 180.69 元(原估 162.71 元)，給予 25 倍本
益比，目標價上修至 4,500 元，維持買進建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 4Q隨著VR小批量出貨，兩家ASIC放量，營運持續季增 
隨著GB300出貨持續強勁 ，VR小批量出貨， 研究部預期奇鋐9月
營收持續月增。展望4Q26，除了VR200板端開始小量出貨外，
Google TPU以及AWS T3水冷也將在4Q開始陸續放量，研究部微
幅調整4Q26營收至632億元(原估622億元)，季增11.9%，年增
32.3%。而VR200的出貨將持續到2Q27，3Q27將開始有小量Rubin 
ultra產品接續。

2. AWS及AMD採用雙面水冷板，散熱產值增加 
根據供應鏈調查， AWS T3水冷以及AMD MI系列將採用雙面式
水冷板，主因採用背面供電(主板空間不夠，記憶體模組移到
主板背面，故兩面都需水冷板散熱)，雙面式水冷板單價將有
望超過3000美元，產值大幅提升。此外，ASIC產品毛利率優於
GPU，公司產能也優先配置給ASIC，故研究部上修27年營收至
3409億元。

3. CDU認證及switch tray規格雜音，對基本面影響極小 
近期散熱有部分雜音：(1) NVIDIA DSX Ready 的CDU認證裡，
取得資格的業者為 LG Electronics、LiquidStack與Vertiv三
間，奇鋐未在首批名單中。研究部認為，目前奇鋐目前主力產
品仍為水冷板等零組件，CDU比較偏向系統級產品 ，27-28年才
會開始小量生產CDU ( 以In row 架構為主)，但目前營收比重
低，影響不大。(2)傳言27年Rubin Ultra 的Switch tray將從
18層改回9層設計，目前仍在討論中，但研究部 對奇鋐的27年
EPS預估，本身就未納入18層規格。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

4. 上修27年EPS預估，目標價上修至4500元  
除了GPU及ASIC需要水冷板之外，周圍相關產品也開始逐漸導
入液冷，如CPU/LPU Rack 、CPO等，奇鋐預估27年水冷營收比
重將超過5成。綜上所述 ，研究部微幅上修2026年營收至2,178
億元(原估2,168億元)， 年增56.0%；EPS 104.65元(原估104.02
元)，並上修2027年營收至3,409億元(原估3,144億元)，年增
56.5%，EPS上修至180.69元(原估162.71元)，給予25倍本益比 ，
目標價上修至4,500元，維持買進建議。

資料來源：CMoney，玉山投顧

黃信智  
simonxzhuang-71976@esunsec.com.tw

研 究  報 告                
中磊(5388.TT) 
產業：電子–通信網路 
三大產品線皆穩健成⻑，惟 2027 年記憶體供需仍持續緊俏 
 投資評價與建議 
看好中磊受惠10G PON、Wi-Fi 7與DOCSIS 4.0網通規格升級以及5G FWA放
量，預估2026/2027年稅後EPS為5.72/7.92元，過往中磊forward PE區間多落
在9.5~15倍，雖然今明年訂單能見度佳，惟考量1H26逐步採用高成本記憶體
生產，同時2027年記憶體供需仍緊俏，故從2027年獲利評價，給予11倍PER。  
 重點評論分析 
Wi-Fi 7規格全面導入，2Q26營收大幅增加，惟毛利率受記憶體漲價承壓：
2Q26營收季增23.9%至206.2億元，係因轉嫁記憶體漲價成本以及Wi-Fi 7規
格產品全面放量 ， 毛利率季減1.36ppts ， 係受記憶體漲價影響 ， 稅後EPS為1.58
元 。 2Q26季底存貨金額較1Q26增加約130億元 ， 其中約80億元為記憶體成品 ，
用於滿足3Q26訂單需求。隨著第三季進入網通出貨旺季，預估3Q26營收季增
9.5%至225.8億元，稅後EPS為1.52元。 
三大產品線均穩健成⻑ ， 惟2027年記憶體供需持續緊俏 ：展望2026~2027年 ，
營收動能來自 ： (1)Broadband CPE產品受惠Fiber PON產品往10G以上規格迭
代，Cable DOCSIS從3.1往4.0升級以及5G FWA出貨量增加；(2)DAA分散式
局端設備目前大型設備商客戶認證時程遞延，預估1Q27將逐步開始出貨，有
利提升後續毛利率表現；(3)Enterprise產品則受惠Wi-Fi 7滲透率提升，目前
Wi-Fi 7比重已達無線產品出貨量之70%。 
 投資風險 
記憶體等零組件漲價與缺料。

| 營運概況  
Wi-Fi 7規格全面導入，2Q26營收大幅增加，惟毛利率受記憶體漲價承壓，進入出貨旺季，3Q26營收持續走升：
2Q26營收季增23.9%至206.2億元 ，營收成⻑中約有⼀半來自記憶體成本轉嫁客戶以及Wi-Fi 7規格產品全面放量 ，
毛利率大幅季減1.36ppts，係受記憶體漲價影響，營業費用率因營收規模擴大而季減1.71ppts至9%，營業利益率
季增0.3ppts至3.3%，稅後EPS為1.58元。資產負債表方面，2Q26季底存貨金額較1Q26增加約130億元，其中約
80億元為記憶體，主要用於滿足3Q26訂單需求。由於記憶體缺貨將造成下游電信營運商客戶無法正常出貨，進而
導致消費者轉往其他同業，將喪失市佔率，故確保「正常交貨」成為網通廠和電信營運商首要目標，公司亦持續
與客戶協商轉嫁成本。隨第三季進入網通出貨旺季，預估3Q26營收季增9.5%至225.8億元，稅後EPS為1.52元。 
三大產品線均穩健成⻑ ， 惟2027年記憶體供需持續緊俏 ：展望2026~2027年，中磊營收動能來自：(1)Broadband 
CPE產品受惠Fiber PON產品往10G以上規格迭代 ， 同時近期中磊領先於同業於SCTE TechExpo26發表50G PON ，
提供客戶下世代光纖產品，另外Cable DOCSIS逐步從3.1往4.0規格升級以及5G FWA出貨量增加；(2)DAA分散式
局端設備透過將大局端設備到⼩節點間改鋪光纖，最後⼀哩路才採用Cable，用於解決訊號衰減與延遲之問題，目
前大型設備商客戶認證時程遞延，預估1Q27將逐步開始出貨，有利提升後續毛利率表現；(3)Enterprise產品受惠
Wi-Fi 7規格滲透率持續提升，目前Wi-Fi 7比重已達無線產品出貨量之70%。綜上所述，預估2026/2027年營收年
增53.9%/14.1%至831/948.4億元，稅後EPS為5.72/7.92元。

壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 訂單滿載與高能見度：信紘科現有訂單達 230 億元，受惠於
AI 與半導體擴建潮，廠務設備與二次配工程訂單滿載，能見度
看至 2028 年。  
 大型統包與海外擴張：業務從單一工程升級至數十億級大型機
電統包，並結合策略夥伴進軍美、日、德、新等海外市場，帶
動營收成長。  
 綠色永續與持續收益：成立專責子公司銳源環境科技，聚焦廢
液處理與資源循環，將一次性工程建置延伸至維修與代操等持
續性收入。

一. 高科技建廠需求強勁，1H26 稅後 EPS 達 7.70 元 
信紘科以特氣特化廠務供應設備系統與二次配裝機工程為主要業
務，近年營運重心與服務範疇持續升級至二次配統包以及機電統
包工程。2025 年高科技產業營收達 59.45 億元，占整體營收高達 
93.56%；2026 年第二季高科技產業營收仍占絕大多數，半導體與
高科技建廠已成為公司最核心且穩定的營收來源

2026 年上半年營收達 32.19 億元；營業毛利 7.49 億元， 毛利率 
23.25% ；營業利益 3.55 億元，稅後淨利 3.79 億元，累計 EPS 達 
7.70 元。目前公司現有在手訂單高達 230 億元，訂 單能見度已直
達 2028 年，整體營運維持強勁成長動能。

二.半導體擴建為主要成長動能，工程與統包範疇擴大 
在廠務與工程服務部分，信紘科目前量產與工程實績包含特氣特
化廠務供應設備、系統建置及二次配管線裝機工程。為進一步提
升單案規模與營運效能，公司將服務領域延伸至二次配統包與大
型機電統包（GC/EPC），使專案金額邁入數十億級規模。後續業
務擴張重點，亦包含綠色製造與循環經濟，於 2025 年 12 月成
立專責子公司「銳源環境科技」，聚焦廢液處理與資源循環，將
一次性建置工程延伸至維修、營運代操及再生產品銷售等多元商
業模式等長期持續性收入。

目前主要服務據點皆配合客戶全球擴產需求建置，公司在台灣擁
有苗栗、台中、台南等據點，憑藉完整的本土供應鏈與人力調度，
能就近支援各大晶圓廠新建、擴建與改建之即時需求（主系統工

三. 預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.76 / 16.83 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 71.17 億元，YoY +12.0%；營業毛利 16.41 
億元，YoY +27.1%，毛利率 23.06%；營業利益 7.92 億元，稅後
純益 7.57 億元，稅後 EPS 14.76 元。展望 2027 年，受惠全球
半導體廠與先進封裝建廠需求熱、在手 230 億元訂單高能見度，
加上大型 GC 統包工程持續認列與海外業務擴張，預計 2027 年
營收 78.29 億元，YoY +10.0%，營業毛利 18.18 億元，毛利率 
23.22% ，營業利益 9.76 億元，稅後純益 8.29 億元，稅後 EPS 為 
16.83 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 330 元 
綜合考量 (1)先進製程相關半導體需求維持強勁 (2) 公司可參與
的工程範圍擴大至海外及機電統包等大型工程，並觀察歷史及同
業本益比區間 ， 目前本益比 16 倍評價偏低 ， 故投資建議為「買進」，
目標價 330 元（20x 2027EPS）。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
信紘科技（Trusval Technology，股票代號：6667）成立於 1995 年，
深耕高科技製造供應鏈布局，並由既有廠務供應設備系統服務，
逐步拓展至機電統包、建廠總承攬及整合型工程解決方案。目前
業務範疇涵蓋廠務設備、製程二次配裝機服務，以及高科技廠房
整合建置與機電統包工程，可依客戶建廠及擴產需求，提供從工
程規劃、設計、採購、施工、測試驗證至系統整合與專案管理的
一站式服務。憑藉跨工種整合、精密製程系統建置及大型專案管
理能力，公司持續提升高附加價值工程案件比重，協助客戶縮短
建廠時程、確保工程品質並提升營運效率。因應全球淨零轉型與
資源循環需求，信紘科技亦積極發展製程機能水設備、廢液處理
與循環經濟解方、資源零廢中心建置等業務，將工程整合能力延
伸至綠色製造與永續基礎建設。

台灣為公司最核心且成熟的基本盤，憑藉超過 30 年高科技產物
工程的深厚經驗，累積了卓越的技術能力與執行實力；得益於台
灣完整的高標準供應鏈以及敏捷的人力與材料調度，能高效支援
客戶新建、擴建及改建廠需求，加上工程需求穩定且執行週期較
短（主系統約 6 至 8 個月，二次配約 4 至 6 個月），構築了
公司長期穩健的營業基礎。

為打造未來成長新引擎，公司順應客戶海外建廠的全球布局與緊
湊時程，將成熟的工程能力輸出海外，信紘科不僅以既有產物工
程為切入點，逐步擴展至純水、廢水、無塵室及機電等領域以擴
大單一案件服務範疇，在非美日等新興區域更結合策略夥伴共同
承攬；同時，公司統包能力持續升級，從早期高技術門檻的二次
配工程，拓展至跨系統二次配統包，進而邁向承接大規模、高整
合度的跨國 GC 與工程管理角色，透過整合台外資源提升接單層
級與案件金額，全面貼近客戶全球建廠需求。

4 
二.產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
A.半導體產業概況  
半導體產業為全球數位經濟之核心基礎建設，近年在人工智慧
（AI）、高效能運算（HPC）及邊緣運算應用加速普及帶動下，產
業需求結構出現顯著變化。先進製程與高密度運算晶片需求持續
提升，使晶圓廠投資動能自庫存調整週期中快速復甦，並進入新
一輪資本支出擴張階段。 根據國際半導體產業協會（SEMI）2025
年發布之預測，全球 300mm 晶圓廠設備支出於 2026 年至 2028 年
間累計將達約 3,740 億美元，其中 2025 年單年度支出將首次突破
1,000 億美元水準。顯示全球半導體建廠投資已由短期循環性波
動，逐步轉為結構性成長態勢。另依 Deloitte（2025）預估，全球
半導體銷售額有機會於 2030 年前後突破 1 兆美元規模，反映產業
長期需求動能仍具成長潛力。

B.半導體產業 ESG 趨勢 
隨淨零排放與國際永續規範逐步落實，ESG 已由合規要求轉為半
導體供應鏈的重要競爭要素。依 SNS Insider（ 2025）預估，全球
半導體廢液處理市場將由 2024 年的 27.2 億美元成長至 2032 年的
49.2 億美元，其中亞太地區受惠於晶圓廠持續擴建與永續轉型，
成長動能可望高於全球平均。在此趨勢下，半導體廠務環保技術
逐步朝 「資源再生、能源轉化、源頭減量」三大方向發展。首先，
硫酸、氨氮廢水及有機溶劑等製程廢液，正由傳統末端處理轉向
回收再生，經純化後重新作為工業級或電子級原料使用，提高資
源循環率並降低廢棄物處置需求。其次，有機廢液處理逐步導入
Waste-to-Energy 技術，將廢液轉化為燃料或能源使用， 以降低外購
能源需求與碳排放。

此外，先進製程亦更加重視源頭減量，透過綠色化學品替代與先
進流體製程，降低強酸、強鹼及水資源使用量。隨 3nm 以下製程
持續發展，兼具降低化學品耗用、廢水負荷與營運成本效益的技
術，導入需求可望提升。 
整體而言，未來半導體廠務市場將由單純設備供應，逐步轉向整

合「資源再生、能源轉化與製程優化」的綜合解決方案，具備相
關技術能力的廠商可望受惠於半導體產業長期淨零轉型需求。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 高科技建廠需求強勁，1H26 稅後 EPS 達 7.70 元 
信紘科以特氣特化廠務供應設備系統與二次配裝機工程為主要業
務，近年營運重心與服務範疇持續升級至二次配統包以及機電統
包工程。2025 年高科技產業營收達 59.45 億元，占整體營收高達 
93.56%；2026 年第二季高科技產業營收仍占絕大多數，半導體與
高科技建廠已成為公司最核心且穩定的營收來源

2026 年上半年營收達 32.19 億元；營業毛利 7.49 億元， 毛利率 
23.25% ；營業利益 3.55 億元，稅後淨利 3.79 億元，累計 EPS 達 
7.70 元。目前公司現有在手訂單高達 230 億元，訂 單能見度已直
達 2028 年，整體營運維持強勁成長動能。

二、預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.76 / 16.83 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 71.17 億元，YoY +12.0%；營業毛利 16.41 
億元，YoY +27.1%，毛利率 23.06%；營業利益 7.92 億元，稅後
純益 7.57 億元，稅後 EPS 14.76 元。展望 2027 年，受惠全球
半導體廠與先進封裝建廠需求熱、在手 230 億元訂單高能見度，
加上大型 GC 統包工程持續認列與海外業務擴張，預計 2027 年
營收 78.29 億元，YoY +10.0%，營業毛利 18.18 億元，毛利率 
23.22% ，營業利益 9.76 億元，稅後純益 8.29 億元，稅後 EPS 為 
16.83 元。

漲價紅利延續；特殊材料蓄勢待發 
 漲價不斷，2H26 獲利將持續跳升：2H26 營運動能持續增溫，主要
受惠 CCL 缺貨，不斷漲價，獲利預期出現跳升。M6 以下、無鉛、
無鹵素及高 TG 等一般材料訂單外溢至騰輝，騰輝 2026 年一般材料
價格 YTD+50%，一般材料已貢獻公司獲利超過一半，9 月份一般材
料再續漲 10~15%；且一般材料產品組合持續轉佳，8 月份起單價與
獲利率較佳之高 TG 產品出貨量成長超過一倍 ， 基本上目前公司所有
產品線毛利率皆達 30%以上，至於特殊材料仍穩定貢獻。 
 深化特殊材料布局 ， 期待新專案驅動中長線成長：截至 3Q26 VT-901
產品營收+70~80%YOY，除了軍工需求倍增，亦有數家國際半導體
設備大廠將 VT-901 用於半導體老化測試板。近期騰輝新增 8～9 個
散熱專案，拓展至 AI 與高功率電源散熱應用，提供後續特殊材料成
長動能。高速材料方面，騰輝亦有送樣下一世代 AI 專案(碳氫 CCL
與 PTFE 皆有送樣)，期待成為 AI 終端客戶二供廠商。 
 買進評等：預期一般材料漲價效益可延續至 2027 年，且若新專案驗
證順利，有望進一步推升產品組合。預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為
15.12/30.86 元，評等買進，目標價 460 元(15x2027EPS)。

個股報告 
騰輝電子-KY 產品主要分一般材
料與特殊材料

騰輝電子-KY 主要從事印刷電路板所需之黏合片、銅箔 基板及鋁基板生產與銷
售，一般材料產品應用於光電板、汽車板等中低階產品，特殊材料主要為散熱
鋁基板，另外亦有著墨軍工航天等利基型領域， 2025 年產品組合：一般材料
41%、特殊材料 53%、其他 6%；2026 年一般材料受惠漲價、缺貨，預估2026
年產品組合：一般材料 53%、特殊材料 41%、其他 6%。騰輝總部位於中國蘇
州，主要生產基地位亦位於中國蘇州(月產能 80 萬張)、江陰(月產能 20 萬張)、
深圳(月產能 10 萬張)與台灣(月產能 10 萬張)， 泰國廠(月產能 10 萬張)則於 2026
年 9 月開始量產。

Sources: Company、Masterlink

漲價不斷，2H26 獲利將持續跳升  騰輝 2H26 營運動能持續增溫，主要受惠 CCL 缺貨，不斷漲價，獲利預期出現
跳升。CCL 上游原料方面，E 布維持逐月漲價，而銅箔供給緊張程度僅次於玻
纖布；CCL 方面，CCL 龍頭廠商將產能集中於 M6 以上高速材料，中低階 CCL
供給明顯收縮，M6 以下、無鉛、無鹵素及高 TG 等一般材料訂單外溢至騰輝，
騰輝 2026 年一般材料價格 YTD+50%，一般材料已貢獻公司獲利超過一半，9
月份一般材料再續漲 10~15%；且一般材料產品組合持續轉佳，8 月份起單價與
獲利率較佳之高 TG 產品出貨量成長超過一倍 ， 基本上目前公司所有產品線毛利
率皆達 30%以上，至於特殊材料仍穩定貢獻；綜上所述，預估3Q26/4Q26 毛利
率將分別提升至 39.8%/41.6%， 推估3Q26/4Q26 EPS 分別為 4.38 元/6.04 元，
合計推估 2026 全年 EPS 為 15.12 元。

深化特殊材料布局，期待新專案
驅動中長線成長 
 騰輝長期聚焦利基型特殊材料市場，並具備自行合成樹脂能力，除了掌握關鍵
配 方 外 ， 亦 可 降 低 原 料 成 本 。PI 方 面 ， 截 至 3Q26 V T-901 產 品 營 收
+70~80%YOY，除了軍工需求倍增，亦有數家國際半導體設備大廠將 VT-901
用於半導體老化測試板，PI 產品毛利率約有 70%高水準；其他高毛利率產品如
不流膠 PP 營收亦有好表現，截至 3Q26 不流膠 PP 營收+50%YOY。另外，騰
輝為全球主要車燈散熱材料供應商，其散熱鋁基板 80%應用於汽車照明，近期
騰輝新增 8～9 個散熱專案，拓展至 AI 與高功率電源散熱應用，提供後續特殊
材料成長動能。高速材料方面，騰輝亦有送樣下一世代 AI 專案(碳氫 CCL 與
PTFE 皆有送樣)，期待成為 AI 終端客戶二供廠商。產能方面，目前稼動率水準
未達滿載以提供訂單彈性；泰國新廠今年 9 月開始試加工，初期由裁切等後段
製程開始，上膠機預計 1H27 進廠裝機，完整產線預計 2027 年到位，目前規劃
至少兩條產線，若全產能運行，預估可增加 10～15%產能，除支應既有客戶需
求外，亦為 2027 年新產品放量預留成長空間；此外，騰輝亦通過 15 億元昆山
擴建案，新產能亦預計於 2027 年展開貢獻。

1 群益投顧 
全福 (6885 TT) 旗下 神 經 營 養 性 角 膜 炎 藥 物 BRM424 ， 美 洲 二 期 臨 床 試 驗 Topline 數據
揭露 ， 14 名 受 試 者 有 7 名在治療 28 天 後 達 到 角 膜 完 全 癒 合 ， 眼 睛 疼 痛 發 生 率 為0% ，
預期定價為 Oxervate 二分之一約 5 萬 美 元 。 商 業 授 權已 取得 2 家 藥 廠 條 件 清 單 ， 規 劃
於 2026 年 底 前 簽 署 授 權 合 約。公 司 認 為 依 美 國 三 期 眼 科 資 產 單 項 市 值3 億至 5 億美元
為 基 準 ， 推 算 旗 下 4 項 資 產 合 計 市 值 為 16 億 美 元 ， 相 較 目 前 市 場 評 價 30 餘 億 新 台 幣
存在落差 。  
研 究 員 ： 吳 健 鉦   
A51755@cim.capital.com.tw

全福新藥 BRM421 已取得 2 家 term sheet ，預計 2026 年底 前 授權 ：  
全福於 9/22/ 2026 說 明 新 藥 研 發 與 授 權 進 度 。 在 神 經 營 養 性 角 膜 炎 藥 物
BRM424 方 面 ， 美 洲 二 期 臨 床 試 驗 中 ，14 名 受 試 者 有 7 名 在 治 療 28 天 後 達
到 角 膜 完 全 癒 合 ， 癒 合 率 為 50.0% ， 眼 睛 疼 痛 發 生 率 為 0% 。 停 止 給 藥 兩 週
後 ， 病 灶 縮 小 中 位 數 由 0.8 毫 米 擴 大 至 1.6 毫 米 ， 角 膜 神 經 敏 感 度 中 位 數 由
0.8 公 分 增 至 1.0 公 分，個 案 敏 感 度 由 2.8 公 分 恢 復 至 5.8 公 分。相 較 現 行 核
准藥物 Oxervate 需負 20 度 保 存 且 每 日 給 藥 6 次， BRM424 可 於 常 溫 保 存 1
個 月 ， 每 日 給 藥 2 至 3 次 ， 預 期 定 價 為 Oxervate 二 分 之 一 約 5 萬美元。  
商 業 授 權 與 法 規 方 面 ， 公 司 已 與 超 過 10 家 藥 廠 簽 署 保 密 協 議 ，7 家 進 行 實
地 查 核 ， 並 取 得 2 家 藥 廠 條 件 清 單 ， 規 劃 於 2026 年 底 前 簽 署 授 權 合 約。公
司 後 續 規 劃 與 美 國 食 品 藥 物 管 理 局 進 行 會 議 ， 確 認80 至 120 人 樞 紐 試 驗 設
計 ， 由 合 作 藥 廠 共 同 執 行 。 美 國 神 經 營 養 性 角 膜 炎 患 者 約6.6 萬 名 ， 現 行 市
售 藥 物 年 使 用 人 數 約 5,000 名 ， 若 用 藥 人 數 提 升 至 3.3 萬 名 ， 使 用 量 將 提 升
為原先之 40 倍 ， 整 體 市 場 規 模 評 估 為 19 億至 22 億 美 元 。 
其他 產 品 線 方 面 ， 青 光 眼 藥 物 BRM411 具備 ROCK1/2 與 MYLK -4 抑制機
制，台 灣 二 期 試 驗 收 案 達 20% 至 30%，無 眼 睛 充 血 通 報，預計於 2Q27 解盲 。
乾 眼 症 藥 物 BRM421 二 期 劑 量 探 索 預 計 於 4Q 26 進 行 期 中 分 析， 2027 年重
啟 三 期 。 濕 式 黃 斑 部 病 變 藥 物BRM412 預 計 於 2027 年 在 澳 洲 啟 動 二 期 臨 床 。 
公司認為 依 美 國 三 期 眼 科 資 產 單 項 市 值 3 億至 5 億 美 元 為 基 準，推 算 旗 下 4
項 資 產 合 計 市 值 為 16 億 美 元，相 較 目 前 市 場 評 價 30 餘 億 新 台 幣 存 在 落 差 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

宏捷科 (8086 TT)  Trading Buy

2H26 展望保守 ， 但1H27 有機會轉佳 ， 評等為Trading Buy 。 
投資建議 ：由於磊晶片、黃金等原材料成本偏高，宏捷科 2H26
獲利表現不易較 2Q26 明顯改善，不過，待新機種相關產品陸續
上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率有機會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳，
故群益對宏捷科的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% ：受惠於手機相關產品
出貨持續暢旺， 2Q26 宏捷科營收 12.98 億元， QoQ+6.23% ，不
過 ， 受到磊晶片 、 黃金等原材料漲價影響 ， 毛利率降低至29.98% ，
營業利益 QoQ-13.40% ，稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% 。  
2H26 獲利展望仍保守，但 1H27 有機會轉佳： 展望 2H26 、2027
年，預估 2H26 營收與 1H26 相去有限，但 2027 年則有成長空
間。若就產品結構而言，首先，雖然手機相關產品雖然必須面
對 2026 年整體手機市場需求因記憶體等零組件漲價、缺料衝擊
而降溫的負面大環境，但持續受惠於中國手機 PA 廠打入韓國
中階手機供應鏈、中國品牌手機高階機種所需 PA 代工地位穩
固，以及美國大客戶 (IC 設計公司 )對中國、韓國旗艦機滲透率
開始提高的衍生商機發揮。  
其次， Wi-Fi 相關產品 2H26 、 2027 年的動能則有三， (1)router 
Wi-Fi PA 訂單數量隨 Wi-Fi 7 市佔率提高而增加， (2) 無人機 PA 
1H26 營收 YoY+194.6% ， 由於客戶家數由 1~2 家增加至 4~5 家，
後續營收成長性仍高， (3) 受惠於美國 IDM 廠逐步退出中國市
場，美國大客戶、中國手機 PA 廠的手機 Wi-Fi PA 陸續打入中
國手機品牌廠的供應鏈，且多數訂單來自旗艦機、高階機種。  
不過，由於中、低階手機舊機種相關產品報價不易調整，旗艦
機、高階手機舊機種的漲價幅度亦難以全數反應原材料成本漲
幅，預估 2H26 宏捷科的毛利率仍受到原材料採購價格偏高影
響，惟待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率即有機
會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就溫室氣體管理策略而言，宏捷科的方向主要有六， (1) 配合政府環保政策
執行溫室氣體盤查， (2) 將空壓機安裝無耗氣排水器，減少運作電能消耗，
(3) 廠房照明設計以自然照明替代電能，並將高耗電之傳統燈具改為 LED 之
省電燈具 (持續改善中 )， (4) 廠內設備逐步汰換 IE3 等級以上之高效能馬達，
(5) 空壓機更換效能較佳的機台及降載， (6) 無塵室氣流循環驅動及空氣過濾
設備降載節能。  
再就溫室氣體排放減量之預算與計畫而言，宏捷科落實的途徑主要有五， (1)
生產程序優化， (2) 廠內節能措施進行， (3) 電力照明之替代及管理， (4) 馬達
汰舊換新 (IE3 等級以上 )， (5) 增設含氟排氣處理設備。  
至於宏捷科溫室氣體排放量減量目標則為針對電能減量以降低間接排放之
溫室氣體數量、減少 PFCs 氣體排放以減少直接排放之溫室氣體數量。  
手機相關產品出貨持續暢旺， 2Q26 稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% ：  
宏捷科為砷化鎵晶圓代工廠，目前總產能為 2 萬片 /月 (以 6 吋晶圓為計算基
礎 )，僅次於同業穩懋 (3105 TT) 的第二大廠，但就現有無塵室空間而言，最
大產能規模可達 4 萬片 /月，換言之，在市場需求增溫時，宏捷科僅需購置、
安裝機台，並通過客戶認證 (部份客戶甚至未要求認證 )後，即可擴大產能規
模。  
宏捷科的產品線包括 PA(power amplifier ，功率放大器 )、 switch/LNA( 開關
/low noise amplifier 低雜訊放大器 )等射頻 (RF，radio frequency) 產品，其中，
PA 多以 HBT 製程生產， switch/LNA 則多以 HEMT 製程生產，此外，宏捷
科亦生產光電 (optical) 產品線，主要為供 3D 感測光源之用的 VCSEL 。若再
就各產品線的終端應用而言，訂單則多來自 Wi-Fi 、手機 (phone) ，近幾年營
收比重分別為 30~60% 、 35~75% 。  
中美晶 (5483 TT) 於 08/2020 參與宏捷科的私募，宏捷科除與中美晶共同進
軍 GaN( 氮化鎵 )領域外，並可藉所取得的資金投入研發，至於中美晶則得以
在宏捷科量產的過程中供應自身生產的 GaN on SiC( 碳化矽基氮化鎵 )材料，
亦即雙方合作將發揮垂直整合綜效。  
2Q26 宏捷科營收 12.98 億元， QoQ+6.23% ，原因在於手機相關產品營收增
加至 9.49 億元， QoQ+7.55% ，不過，受到磊晶片 (epi wafer) 、黃金等原材料
漲價影響，毛利率降低至 29.98%(1Q26 為 34.63%) ，營業利益 QoQ-13.40% ，
稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% 。  
1H26 宏捷科營收 25.21 億元，為歷史次高， YoY+44.36% ，其中，手機相關
產品營收 18.32 億元， YoY+56.16% ，原因有二， (1) 中國手機 PA 廠打入韓
國中階手機供應鏈，且自 2026 年開始大量出貨 (過去中、高階手機用 PA 多
由美國業者供應 )， (2) 中國手機 PA 廠供中國手機品牌業者高階機種使用的
產品多仍交由宏捷科代工 (搭載於中、低階手機的 PA 則已積極轉單至中國
同業 )。  
2H26 毛利率仍受原物料成本偏高影響，但 1H27 獲利展望有機會轉佳：  
展望 2H26 、 2027 年，預估 2H26 營收與 1H26 相去有限，但 2027 年則有成

3 群益投顧 
長空間。若就產品結構而言，首先，雖然手機相關產品雖然必須面對 2026
年整體手機市場需求因記憶體等零組件漲價、缺料衝擊而降溫的負面大環
境，但持續受惠於中國手機 PA 廠打入韓國中階手機供應鏈、中國品牌手機
高階機種所需 PA 代工地位穩固，以及美國大客戶 (IC 設計公司 )對中國、韓
國旗艦機滲透率開始提高的衍生商機發揮。  
其次， Wi-Fi 相關產品 2H26 、 2027 年的動能則有三， (1)router Wi-Fi PA 訂
單數量隨 Wi-Fi 7 市佔率提高而增加 ，(2) 無人機 PA 1H26 營收 YoY+194.6% ，
由於客戶家數由 1~2 家增加至 4~5 家，後續營收成長性仍高， (3) 受惠於美
國 IDM 廠逐步退出中國市場，美國大客戶、中國手機 PA 廠的手機 Wi-Fi 
PA 陸續打入中國手機品牌廠的供應鏈，且多數訂單來自旗艦機、高階機種。
最後，在新產品的部份，包括 datacom 用 VCSEL( 後續亦將開發 PD 及更高
波長的產品 )、濾波器、消費型 LiDAR( 終端應用如車用、掃地機器人、割草
機等 )均已量產， solar cell 則將於 2026 年年底量產，惟對宏捷科的營收貢獻
程度則均尚待觀察。  
不過，由於中、低階手機舊機種相關產品報價不易調整，旗艦機、高階手機
舊機種的漲價幅度亦難以全數反應原材料成本漲幅，預估 2H26 宏捷科的毛
利率仍受到原材料採購價格偏高影響，惟待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預
估 1H27 毛利率有機會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳。  
雖 2H26 獲利展望仍保守，但 1H27 有機會轉佳，群益對宏捷科的投資建議
為 Trading Buy ：  
由於磊晶片、黃金等原材料成本偏高，宏捷科 2H26 獲利表現不易較 2Q26
明顯改善，不過，待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率有機會
回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳，故群益對宏捷科的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 167: AI驅動高速互聯，矽光子落地，CPO續章 202609

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI驅動高速互聯，矽光子落地，CPO續章 202609.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源

### Page 2

2【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
結論
⚫ AI算力需求推升高速光互聯市場。美國四大CSP持續擴大AI資料中心建置，為帶動高速光
模組需求成長的主要動能。現階段高速光模組出貨仍以800G為主，1.6T預計自2027年逐步
放量，也帶動矽光(SiPh)模組滲透率提高。
⚫ 矽光模組非新興產品，外型仍沿用既有可插拔光模組規格，但相較傳統光模組，SiPh具備
元件高度整合及晶圓級量產優勢，隨高速傳輸所需而獲廣泛採用。然而，雷射晶片受限於
上游InP基板缺貨而供應吃緊。為確保貨源，部分業者已透過長約或預付款方式鎖定供應，
使掌握貨源者在市場上更具競爭優勢。
⚫ CPO現階段仍面臨光耦合、測試與量產等製程挑戰；對CSP而言，CPO改變其既有可插拔光
模組的維修與更換模式，加上NPO、XPO等替代架構持續發展，使CPO雖已進入量產階段
，短期滲透率仍偏低。展望未來，傳輸頻寬持續提升，為降低傳輸損耗與功率，光引擎將
逐步靠近交換器ASIC或加速卡，CPO仍具導入需求。此外，NVIDIA持續擴大NVLink

Fusion生態系，擴大CPO潛在應用市場，惟規模部署時程仍待帶動頻寬需求的新應用興起。
⚫ 相關個股：聯亞(3081)、IET-KY(4971)、寰宇-KY(4991)、聯鈞(3450)、訊芯-KY(6451)、華
星光(4979)、光聖(6442)、波若威(3163)、上詮(3363)、大立光(3008)

### Page 4

AI算力需求推升高速光互聯市場

### Page 5

5【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
AI DC建置，光通訊需求持續成長
• 生成式AI浪潮帶動美國CSP進入DC建置競賽，2023年仍為投資初期，2024年CapEx明顯加速
，預期2026年美國四大CSP—Amazon、Google、微軟、Meta ，CapEx合計增至7,500億美元，
帶動高速光互聯(networking)需求。
• Amazon更指出，預期AWS算力供不應求情況將延續至2027年，且2028年需求能見度已高。
• 2022年底ChatGPT推出帶動生成式AI浪潮，惟當時COVID-19疫情剛結束，Amazon和Meta因
疫情期間大幅擴張基礎建設而下調2023年CapEx，使四大CSP整體CapEx較2022年略低。
資料來源：福邦投顧彙整

### Page 6

6【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
預估2031年光模組市場營收將達1,120億美元
資料來源：Yole, 福邦投顧彙整
• 市調機構預估，至2031年光模組市場營收將達1,120億美元，CAGR成長30%。AI DC為營收
貢獻主力， 佔比達9成。
• 2025年合計資料中心與電信商的光模組營收為230億美元，當時兩者營收分別為180億與50億
美元，差異有限；2031年資料中心的光模組營收達1,010億，電信商僅增為110億美元。
圖2、2025與2031年光模組市場規模比較

### Page 7

7【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
光互連應用由機櫃延伸至跨資料中心
資料來源：Ciena, 福邦投顧彙整
圖3、資料中心Scale Up、Scale Out與Scale Across架構
• 目前光通訊產品出貨集中於Scale Up與Scale Out應用；隨資料中心建置規模擴大及耗電量增加
，受限於電力與土地供給，資料中心朝分散式建置發展，並透過光纖串聯多座資料中心以進行
共同運算，帶動Scale Across需求。
• Scale Up為機櫃內元件與設備互連；Scale Out為資料中心內不同機櫃間互連；Scale Across為
資料中心互連。

### Page 8

8【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
依傳輸距離採用不同光互聯技術
資料來源：Ciena, 福邦投顧彙整
• Scale Up傳輸距離較短，以成本較低的主動銅纜(ACC)為主；隨傳輸速率增加，銅線難以維
持訊號品質，便會採用光纖兩端配置光收發模組的主動光纜(AOC)。
• Scale Out主流採用IMDD可插拔(pluggable)光模組。
• NPO和CPO為應用於Scale Out和Scale up的新技術，藉縮短光引擎與交換器ASIC距離以降低
高速傳輸的電訊號損耗。NPO的光引擎鄰近交換器ASIC擺放；CPO的光引擎與交換器ASIC
共同封裝。
• Scale Across傳輸距離約20公里以上，需採用相干光(coherent)技術來維持訊號品質，供應商
相對有限，以歐美系業者為主。

AI資料中心的光互聯台廠供應鏈
• AI DC 高速互連升級，帶動 AOC/ACC 與800G/1.6T可插拔光模組需求成長，並推升矽光
(SiPh)模組滲透率。台廠AOC供應商包括佳必琪與源傑。
• 台廠受惠範圍由光模組元件與封裝，進一步延伸至CW Laser、光學次模組封裝及 PIC 代工
• 高密度光纖佈線需求同步增加，帶動 MPO/Jumper、Fiber Harness、Shuffle Module 等產品需
求，台廠供應商包括光聖、波若威、佳必琪、貿聯-KY。

### Page 11

11【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
光模組朝高速與高密度整合演進
• AI DC帶動高速光模組需求，2026年以400G、800G為主，1.6T開始放量；2027年1.6T出貨量
將明顯提升，並預期2028年導入3.2T。市調機構LightCounting預估，隨800G、1.6T等高速光
模組需求增加，矽光模組滲透率將提升，2026年矽光模組銷售額YoY成長110%，占整體光模
組市場約50%。
• 光模組速率提升是透過增加光通道或提高單通道速率來實現，以每通道200G為例，800G需4
個光通道，1.6T則增至8個，通道數增加所需的調變器、PD等元件數量也隨之增加，使耦合
及封裝複雜度提高，凸顯矽光模組的整合優勢
資料來源：LightCounting，千才，福邦投顧彙整

### Page 12

12【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽光PIC高度整合光元件並以半導體製程量產
• 矽光模組是指搭載矽光PIC的光模組，矽光PIC透過半導體製程，將調變器、波導等光學功能
整合於晶片，減少離散元件組裝及光學對準步驟，並有利於晶圓級量產。
• 2016年英特爾即推出矽光子光模組，惟早期高速傳輸需求有限，矽光PIC整合效益尚不顯著；
隨800G、1.6T以上高速光模組放量，通道數及光元件數量增加，矽光PIC整合優勢浮現。
• 現階段矽光模組仍沿用沿用OSFP、QSFP-DD既有可插拔光模組外型。
• 過去光通訊PIC多採III-V族材料，雷射與光元件可單晶整合，但市場規模較小且製程專用，多
以IDM方式生產；矽光PIC採CMOS相容製程，可導入成熟矽晶圓代工模式，實現規模量產與
降低成本，然無法與雷射整合。
資料來源：博通，福邦投顧彙整
圖7、傳統光模組與矽光子光模組架構比較

### Page 13

13【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽光PIC需求擴大，專業分工成形
• 矽光PIC晶圓代工目前以Tower半導體量產規模及客戶能見度最高；聯電亦積極切入，2025年
取得比利時半導體研究機構imec的12吋矽光子製程技術授權，2026年於新加坡12吋廠量產
1.6T PIC，預計2027年向更多客戶開放矽光代工平台。
• 除英特爾外，其餘具備矽光PIC設計能力業者並未單獨銷售PIC，而是將PIC與EIC、DSP整合
為光引擎(optical engine)後出售。
• 2023年英特爾出售光模組事業予Jabil，專注於提供整合雷射的矽光PIC。
資料來源：福邦投顧彙整

板實際供給量。

預留。

### Page 18

CPO發展趨勢與導入挑戰

### Page 19

19【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CPO於3.2T世代有望加速滲透
• 台積電表示CPO模組將於2026年下半年量產，惟依供應鏈資訊，初期出貨量仍低、市場規模
有限。市調機構預估，CPO光模組出貨量將自2027年起明顯成長，2030年達約6,000萬顆；
滲透率部分，將由2027年的11%提升至2028年的19%，2030年進一步達38%。
• 業界普遍認為，傳輸速率進入3.2T世代後，CPO在功耗與頻寬密度上的優勢將更為明顯。博
通也表示，CPO的光互聯耗電量較可插拔光模組減少70%。
資料來源：Lightcounting，Dell’Oro, 福邦投顧彙整
圖10、CPO出貨量與滲透率預估

3M 10M
32M

### Page 20

20【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CPO推動光電共封裝，FAU成光耦合關鍵元件
• CPO為光電共封裝架構，將 PIC與EIC 整合為光引擎(OE)，再與Switch ASIC共封裝於同一載
板，目標降低電訊號傳輸距離與功耗。
• 雷射仍放置於交換器機箱前面板，且維持可插拔，以方便更換。
• 目前台積電以COUPE平台整合PIC與EIC成光引擎，然提高光耦合效率、降低光進入 PIC 的
損耗，成為CPO主要瓶頸之一，也使 FAU 成為關鍵元件。
資料來源：Photonics Spectra, 福邦投顧彙整
圖11、CPO主要元件
矽光模組

### Page 21

21【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
FAU難以晶片化，攸關CPO光耦合效率與量產時程
• 目前台積電COUPE平台優先採用光柵(grating)耦合架構，FAU以黏膠固定於光學引擎上
方。光線平行射出，需以反射鏡導入PIC；COUPE端利用矽透鏡將光聚焦至光柵耦合器
，也配置金屬反射鏡回收散射光，提升光耦合效率。
• 光耦合效率攸關光學元件設計與光纖精密對位。FAU除涉及光學設計外，光纖亦需精準
置入V-groove，並完成固定、端面研磨與對位封裝，工序繁雜而被視為CPO規模量產瓶
頸之一。
• FAU投入業者主要包括線材廠、傳統FAU業者及光學元件廠。例如，光聖旗下合聖推出
以Metalens搭配反射鏡的可拆卸式FAU；大立光則推出配置80顆微型鏡頭、整合反射鏡
與稜鏡功能的PMLA，並進一步布局多層堆疊光纖。線材廠方面，以波若威、佳必琪與
貿聯為代表；傳統FAU業者則以上銓為代表。
資料來源：TSMC, 福邦投顧彙整
圖12、TSMC COUPE的FAU光耦合架構
大立光、合聖針對反射鏡推出解決方案

### Page 22

22【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
FAU技術升級聚焦提升耦光效率與縮短對位時間
• FAU主要發展方向為可拆卸設計、多層光纖堆疊與被動對位。其中，可拆卸式方案以美、日
業者布局較積極，主要考量FAU或光纖生損壞可直接更換，避免拆除整組光學引擎，降低維
修難度及交換器損壞風險。
• 目前FAU多採主動對位，透過反覆微調位置並量測光功率，找出最佳耦合點後固定，雖可補
償製造與組裝誤差，但組裝時間長。被動對位仰賴高精度製程與定位結構，將精度要求前移
，以簡化後段組裝並提升量產效率
資料來源：各業者, 福邦投顧彙整
圖13、FAU正發展可拆卸、多層堆疊及被動對準
日商Senko的可拆卸FAU
Teramount雙層光纖設計使
光纖量從單層的32條增為
64條。
Teramount與高塔半導體合作開發Bump-ready wafer，
即在PIC晶圓階段預先製作光學耦合與對位結構。

### Page 23

23【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
NPO持續演進 成為Scale Up另一選擇
• NPO被視為CPO折衷方案，光引擎置於鄰近交換器ASIC，降低傳輸損耗且保留維修彈性。
• 華為認為NPO具備提供400G/lane的潛力，3.2T世代未必需全面導入CPO。
• 博通、Marvell、Coherent皆已展示或推出NPO方案，光源架構部分，Marvell主打無DSP、配
置CW雷射；博通展示VCSEL方案，Coherent同時推出VCSEL和CW方案，VCSEL功耗低，
晶片尺寸小，可高密度排列，並與光引擎整合封裝，然傳輸距離有限，適合Scale-up應用。
• 被視為Marvell合作業者的華星光，預期2027年下半開始量產1.6T NPO模組，2028年出貨量
體將較為顯著。
資料來源：華為, 福邦投顧彙整
圖14、NPO與CPO技術路徑比較

### Page 24

24【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
XPO重塑可插拔模組 挑戰CPO的Scale Out市場
• 美網通業者Arista於2026年成立XPO MSA，並展示首代支援200G/lane，頻寬12.8T的可插拔
XPO模組，後續將發展400G/lane、單模組頻寬25.6T，惟量產與規模商用時程尚未明朗。
XPO MSA成員橫跨CSP、ASIC、光元件與光模組供應鏈，包括微軟、Marvell、Coherent、
Lumentum及新易盛等。Arista為Ethernet交換器設備商，主導XPO規格與生態系發展。
• XPO主打Scale-out應用，強調單一XPO模組可取代8顆OSFP，在有限機箱空間內提高整體傳
輸頻寬。由於XPO採全新模組機構，交換器須配合重新設計供電、散熱及相關介面，目前規
格制定與系統平台仍以Arista為主；儘管XPO MSA讓CSP在光模組供應商選擇上更具彈性，
但在其他交換器業者尚未跟進前，導入XPO亦可能意味對Arista交換器平台的更高依賴。
資料來源：福邦投顧彙整

圖15、XPO模組架構與應用示意
